## Guidance Document V3
*Considering: Data Sheet V3 + To Do List V3 + Detailed Procedure V2*

### Purpose

This document orients the agent executing Instance 1 Step 2 to derive software requirements from the approved Problem Statement.

### Position in Workflow

| Instance | Step | Input | Output |
|----------|------|-------|--------|
| Instance 0 | Step 2 | Abstract Problem Statement | Abstract Requirements |
| **Instance 1** | **Step 2** | **Software Problem Statement** | **Software Requirements** |
| Instance N | Step 2 | Domain Problem Statement | Domain Requirements |

Instance 1 Step 2 produces the requirements specification for the structured reasoning software system.

### Inheritance from Instance 0

Instance 0 Step 2 established:
- Requirement schema (id, statement, category, priority, source)
- Three categories (FR, NFR, CR)
- Derivation from stakeholders, constraints, criteria
- Coverage requirements
- Traceability requirements

Instance 1 **specializes**:
- Adds Interface Requirements (IR) as fourth category
- Adds component allocation (software-specific)
- Adds acceptance criteria linkage
- Adds software-specific subcategories
- Adds testability methods specific to software

### The Four Requirement Categories

| Category | Prefix | Question Answered | Derived From |
|----------|--------|-------------------|--------------|
| Functional | FR | What does the system do? | Core problem, stakeholder needs, scope items |
| Non-Functional | NFR | How well does it do it? | Stakeholder concerns, soft constraints |
| Constraint | CR | What limits must it respect? | Hard constraints |
| Interface | IR | How do components interact? | Integration points |

### Derivation Patterns

**Pattern 1: Stakeholder Need → Functional Requirement**
```
Source: SH-001 (End User)
Need: "Execute workflows through intuitive interface"

Derivation:
  - The user needs to execute workflows
  - This requires a user-facing interface
  - The interface must be intuitive (usable)

Requirement:
  FR-004: "The system shall provide a web-based interface that allows 
           users to initiate, monitor, and interact with structured 
           reasoning workflows"
  Component: [Frontend]
  Priority: should
  Testability: demonstration - execute workflow via UI
```

**Pattern 2: Stakeholder Concern → Non-Functional Requirement**
```
Source: SH-001 (End User)
Concern: "System responsiveness; workflow clarity"

Derivation:
  - User is concerned about responsiveness
  - This is a performance quality attribute
  - Need measurable criterion

Requirement:
  NFR-001: "The system shall begin streaming agent responses to the 
            user interface within 500ms of response generation"
  Subcategory: performance
  Component: [API, Frontend]
  Priority: should
  Metric: response latency ≤ 500ms
  Testability: test - measure latency under load
```

**Pattern 3: Hard Constraint → Constraint Requirement**
```
Source: HC-004
Statement: "Pass 2 must not advance without explicit human approval"

Derivation:
  - This is a behavioral constraint
  - It's non-negotiable (hard constraint)
  - Priority must be "must"

Requirement:
  CR-004: "The system shall prevent any Pass 2 step from advancing to 
           the next step until explicit human approval is received"
  Subcategory: behavior
  Component: [Orchestration, API]
  Priority: must
  Violation: Human oversight bypassed; trust violation
  Testability: test - attempt advance without approval, verify blocked
```

**Pattern 4: Integration Point → Interface Requirement**
```
Source: IP-002
Name: "API → Frontend (Streaming)"
Protocol: SSE

Derivation:
  - Components must communicate via SSE
  - Data flows from API to Frontend
  - Real-time streaming required

Requirement:
  IR-002: "The API shall stream agent responses to the frontend using 
           Server-Sent Events (SSE) protocol"
  From: API
  To: Frontend
  Protocol: SSE
  Data: Agent responses, state updates
  Priority: must
  Testability: test - verify SSE connection and data flow
```

### Component Allocation

Every requirement must specify which components are affected:

| Component | Technology | Affected By |
|-----------|------------|-------------|
| Frontend | Next.js, assistant-ui | UI requirements, streaming display |
| API | FastAPI | Endpoints, request handling, SSE |
| Orchestration | LangGraph | Workflow, state, gates, checkpoints |
| Persistence | PostgreSQL | Storage, queries, recovery |
| LLM | Claude API | Reasoning, generation |
| Observability | LangSmith/Langfuse | Tracing, debugging |

### Priority Assignment

| Source Type | Default Priority | Elevate To |
|-------------|------------------|------------|
| Core problem | must | — |
| Stakeholder need | should | must if linked to success criterion |
| Stakeholder concern | should | must if safety-related |
| Hard constraint | must | — |
| Soft constraint | could | — |
| Scope item | should | must if linked to success criterion |
| Integration point | must | — |

### Acceptance Criteria Attachment

Success criteria from Step 1 should be attached to relevant requirements:

| Criterion | Attach To | As |
|-----------|-----------|-----|
| CRT-001: 10 steps executable | FR (workflow) | Acceptance criterion |
| CRT-002: Pass 1 autonomous | FR (two-pass) | Acceptance criterion |
| CRT-003: Gates block | CR-004 | Acceptance criterion |
| CRT-004: Checkpoint recovery | FR (persistence) | Acceptance criterion |
| CRT-005: Traceability chain | FR (traceability) | Acceptance criterion |
| CRT-006: Instance inheritance | FR (hierarchy) | Acceptance criterion |
| CRT-007: Visible reasoning | CR-006 | Acceptance criterion |

### Coverage Requirements

**Full coverage required:**
- Every stakeholder need → ≥1 FR
- Every hard constraint → ≥1 CR
- Every in-scope item → ≥1 FR
- Every integration point → ≥1 IR

**Partial coverage acceptable:**
- Stakeholder concerns (warning if missing)
- Success criteria attachment (warning if missing)

### Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Example | Remedy |
|--------------|---------|--------|
| Implementation leakage | "Use useState hook" | Say WHAT, not HOW |
| Untestable requirement | "Be user-friendly" | Add measurable criterion |
| Missing traceability | FR without source | All requirements trace to Step 1 |
| Orphan source | Stakeholder without requirements | Add requirements or justify |
| Compound requirement | "Shall do X and Y and Z" | Split into separate requirements |
| Duplicate requirement | Same capability twice | Merge or differentiate |

### Quality Checklist

Before presenting for human review:

1. [ ] All stakeholder needs have ≥1 FR
2. [ ] All stakeholder concerns have ≥1 NFR
3. [ ] All hard constraints have exactly 1 CR
4. [ ] All scope items have ≥1 FR
5. [ ] All integration points have ≥1 IR
6. [ ] All requirements have testability defined
7. [ ] All requirements have component allocation
8. [ ] All CR have priority = must
9. [ ] All requirements have rationale
10. [ ] Success criteria attached to relevant FR
11. [ ] No duplicate statements
12. [ ] No contradictions
13. [ ] Total count in range [20, 60]

---

## Detailed Procedure V3
*Considering: Data Sheet V3 + To Do List V3 + Guidance Document V3*

### State Machine
```
STATES = {RECEIVED, ANALYZING, DERIVING, VALIDATING, REVIEWING, COMPLETE}
INITIAL = RECEIVED
TERMINAL = COMPLETE

TRANSITIONS:
  RECEIVED    → ANALYZING   : input_valid()
  ANALYZING   → DERIVING    : seeds_extracted()
  DERIVING    → VALIDATING  : requirements_complete()
  VALIDATING  → DERIVING    : validation_failed()
  VALIDATING  → REVIEWING   : validation_passed()
  REVIEWING   → DERIVING    : rejected(feedback)
  REVIEWING   → VALIDATING  : modify(changes)
  REVIEWING   → COMPLETE    : approved()

HUMAN_GATES = {REVIEWING}
```

---

### Phase 1: Reception [RECEIVED]

**Entry:** Instance 1 Step 1 Output provided

**Procedure:**
```
1.1 VERIFY Step 1 Output:
    ASSERT step_1.metadata.state == "COMPLETE"
    ASSERT step_1.metadata.approval.decision == "APPROVE"

1.2 LOAD fields:
    
    core_problem = step_1.core_problem
    # {statement, differs_from_stated, difference_explanation}
    
    stakeholders = step_1.stakeholders
    # 5 items: SH-001 to SH-005
    # Each has: id, name, type, primary_need, primary_concern
    
    hard_constraints = step_1.hard_constraints
    # 6 items: HC-001 to HC-006
    # Each has: id, statement, category, violation_consequence
    
    soft_constraints = step_1.soft_constraints
    # 1 item: SC-001
    
    scope_in = step_1.scope.in_scope
    # 10 items: IN-001 to IN-010
    
    success_criteria = step_1.success_criteria
    # 7 items: CRT-001 to CRT-007
    
    integration_points = step_1.integration_points
    # 6 items: IP-001 to IP-006
    
    technology_stack = step_1.technology_stack
    # 6 layers

1.3 INITIALIZE output:
    Instance1Step2Output = {
      metadata: {...},
      requirements: {functional: [], non_functional: [], constraint: [], interface: []},
      traceability: {...},
      coverage_summary: {...},
      summary: {...}
    }

1.4 SET state: ANALYZING

TRANSITION → ANALYZING
```

---

### Phase 2: Seed Extraction [ANALYZING]

**Entry:** Input validated

**Procedure:**
```
2.1 EXTRACT core problem seeds:

    core_seeds = [
      {
        text: "implement structured reasoning methodology",
        category: "FR",
        priority: "must",
        source: {type: "problem", id: "core_problem", aspect: "statement"}
      },
      {
        text: "10-step workflow execution",
        category: "FR",
        priority: "must",
        source: {type: "problem", id: "core_problem", aspect: "statement"}
      },
      {
        text: "human oversight at gates",
        category: "FR",
        priority: "must",
        source: {type: "problem", id: "core_problem", aspect: "statement"}
      }
    ]

2.2 EXTRACT stakeholder seeds:

    FOR EACH sh IN stakeholders:
      need_seed = {
        text: sh.primary_need,
        category: "FR",
        priority: "should",
        source: {type: "stakeholder", id: sh.id, aspect: "need"}
      }
      concern_seed = {
        text: sh.primary_concern,
        category: "NFR",
        priority: "should",
        source: {type: "stakeholder", id: sh.id, aspect: "concern"}
      }
      stakeholder_seeds.append(need_seed)
      stakeholder_seeds.append(concern_seed)

2.3 EXTRACT constraint seeds:

    FOR EACH hc IN hard_constraints:
      constraint_seed = {
        text: hc.statement,
        category: "CR",
        subcategory: hc.category,
        priority: "must",
        violation: hc.violation_consequence,
        source: {type: "constraint", id: hc.id}
      }
      constraint_seeds.append(constraint_seed)
    
    FOR EACH sc IN soft_constraints:
      soft_seed = {
        text: sc.statement,
        category: "NFR",
        priority: "could",
        source: {type: "soft_constraint", id: sc.id}
      }
      soft_seeds.append(soft_seed)

2.4 EXTRACT scope seeds:

    FOR EACH item IN scope_in:
      scope_seed = {
        text: item.item,
        category: "FR",
        priority: "should",
        source: {type: "scope", id: item.id, aspect: "item"}
      }
      scope_seeds.append(scope_seed)

2.5 EXTRACT integration seeds:

    FOR EACH ip IN integration_points:
      integration_seed = {
        name: ip.name,
        from: ip.from_component,
        to: ip.to_component,
        protocol: ip.protocol,
        data: ip.data_exchanged,
        category: "IR",
        priority: "must",
        source: {type: "integration", id: ip.id}
      }
      integration_seeds.append(integration_seed)

2.6 EXTRACT criteria for attachment:

    FOR EACH crt IN success_criteria:
      criterion_seed = {
        id: crt.id,
        text: crt.criterion,
        verification: crt.verification_method,
        use_as: "acceptance_criteria"
      }
      criteria_seeds.append(criterion_seed)

2.7 CONSOLIDATE all seeds:

    all_seeds = {
      core: core_seeds,           # 3
      stakeholder: stakeholder_seeds,  # 10 (5 needs + 5 concerns)
      constraint: constraint_seeds,    # 6
      soft: soft_seeds,           # 1
      scope: scope_seeds,         # 10
      integration: integration_seeds,  # 6
      criteria: criteria_seeds    # 7
    }

TRANSITION → DERIVING
```

---

### Phase 3: Requirement Derivation [DERIVING]

**Entry:** Seeds extracted

**Procedure:**
```
3.1 DERIVE Functional Requirements:

    fr_counter = 0
    FR = []
    
    # From core problem (3 seeds → ~3-5 FR)
    FOR EACH seed IN all_seeds.core:
      fr_counter++
      fr = {
        id: "FR-" + pad(fr_counter, 3),
        statement: derive_statement(seed),
        category: "functional",
        priority: seed.priority,
        source: seed.source,
        component: determine_components(seed),
        rationale: derive_rationale(seed),
        testability: {
          method: determine_test_method(seed),
          description: derive_test_description(seed)
        },
        acceptance_criteria: []
      }
      FR.append(fr)
    
    # From stakeholder needs (5 seeds → 5 FR)
    FOR EACH seed IN all_seeds.stakeholder WHERE aspect == "need":
      fr_counter++
      fr = derive_fr_from_need(seed, fr_counter)
      FR.append(fr)
    
    # From scope items (10 seeds → 10 FR, some may consolidate)
    FOR EACH seed IN all_seeds.scope:
      # Check if already covered by core/stakeholder FR
      IF NOT covered_by_existing(seed, FR):
        fr_counter++
        fr = derive_fr_from_scope(seed, fr_counter)
        FR.append(fr)
      ELSE:
        # Add traceability to existing FR
        existing.source.also_from.append(seed.source)

3.2 DERIVE Non-Functional Requirements:

    nfr_counter = 0
    NFR = []
    
    # From stakeholder concerns (5 seeds → 5 NFR)
    FOR EACH seed IN all_seeds.stakeholder WHERE aspect == "concern":
      nfr_counter++
      nfr = {
        id: "NFR-" + pad(nfr_counter, 3),
        statement: derive_nfr_statement(seed),
        category: "non_functional",
        subcategory: classify_nfr(seed),  # performance, reliability, etc.
        priority: seed.priority,
        source: seed.source,
        component: determine_components(seed),
        rationale: derive_rationale(seed),
        testability: {
          method: determine_nfr_test(seed),
          description: derive_test_description(seed)
        },
        metric: derive_metric_if_applicable(seed)
      }
      NFR.append(nfr)
    
    # From soft constraints (1 seed → 1 NFR)
    FOR EACH seed IN all_seeds.soft:
      nfr_counter++
      nfr = derive_nfr_from_soft(seed, nfr_counter)
      NFR.append(nfr)

3.3 DERIVE Constraint Requirements:

    cr_counter = 0
    CR = []
    
    # From hard constraints (6 seeds → 6 CR)
    FOR EACH seed IN all_seeds.constraint:
      cr_counter++
      cr = {
        id: "CR-" + pad(cr_counter, 3),
        statement: derive_cr_statement(seed),
        category: "constraint",
        subcategory: seed.subcategory,
        priority: "must",  # Always must for constraints
        source: seed.source,
        component: determine_components(seed),
        rationale: "Derived from hard constraint " + seed.source.id,
        testability: {
          method: determine_cr_test(seed),
          description: derive_test_description(seed)
        },
        violation_consequence: seed.violation
      }
      CR.append(cr)

3.4 DERIVE Interface Requirements:

    ir_counter = 0
    IR = []
    
    # From integration points (6 seeds → 6 IR)
    FOR EACH seed IN all_seeds.integration:
      ir_counter++
      ir = {
        id: "IR-" + pad(ir_counter, 3),
        statement: derive_ir_statement(seed),
        category: "interface",
        priority: seed.priority,
        source: seed.source,
        from_component: seed.from,
        to_component: seed.to,
        protocol: seed.protocol,
        data_format: derive_data_format(seed),
        rationale: "Derived from integration point " + seed.source.id,
        testability: {
          method: "test",
          description: "Verify " + seed.protocol + " communication between " + seed.from + " and " + seed.to
        }
      }
      IR.append(ir)

3.5 ATTACH acceptance criteria:

    criteria_mapping = {
      "CRT-001": ["FR covering workflow execution"],
      "CRT-002": ["FR covering Pass 1 autonomy"],
      "CRT-003": ["CR-004"],
      "CRT-004": ["FR covering checkpoint recovery"],
      "CRT-005": ["FR covering traceability"],
      "CRT-006": ["FR covering instance inheritance"],
      "CRT-007": ["CR-006"]
    }
    
    FOR EACH crt IN all_seeds.criteria:
      target_reqs = find_matching_requirements(crt, FR, CR)
      FOR EACH req IN target_reqs:
        req.acceptance_criteria.append(crt.text)

TRANSITION → VALIDATING
```

---

### Phase 4: Validation [VALIDATING]

**Entry:** Requirements derived

**Procedure:**
```
4.1 BUILD forward traceability:

    forward_trace = []
    FOR EACH req IN (FR + NFR + CR + IR):
      forward_trace.append({
        requirement_id: req.id,
        source_type: req.source.type,
        source_id: req.source.id,
        derivation: req.source.aspect or "direct"
      })

4.2 BUILD backward traceability:

    # Stakeholder coverage
    stakeholder_coverage = []
    FOR EACH sh IN stakeholders:
      need_reqs = FILTER FR WHERE source.id == sh.id AND source.aspect == "need"
      concern_reqs = FILTER NFR WHERE source.id == sh.id AND source.aspect == "concern"
      stakeholder_coverage.append({
        stakeholder_id: sh.id,
        stakeholder_name: sh.name,
        need: sh.primary_need,
        concern: sh.primary_concern,
        need_requirements: [r.id FOR r IN need_reqs],
        concern_requirements: [r.id FOR r IN concern_reqs]
      })
    
    # Constraint coverage
    constraint_coverage = []
    FOR EACH hc IN hard_constraints:
      matching_cr = FILTER CR WHERE source.id == hc.id
      constraint_coverage.append({
        constraint_id: hc.id,
        statement: hc.statement,
        requirements: [r.id FOR r IN matching_cr]
      })
    
    # Scope coverage
    scope_coverage = []
    FOR EACH item IN scope_in:
      matching_fr = FILTER FR WHERE source.id == item.id OR source.also_from contains item.id
      scope_coverage.append({
        scope_id: item.id,
        item: item.item,
        requirements: [r.id FOR r IN matching_fr]
      })
    
    # Integration coverage
    integration_coverage = []
    FOR EACH ip IN integration_points:
      matching_ir = FILTER IR WHERE source.id == ip.id
      integration_coverage.append({
        integration_id: ip.id,
        name: ip.name,
        requirements: [r.id FOR r IN matching_ir]
      })
    
    # Criteria coverage
    criteria_coverage = []
    FOR EACH crt IN success_criteria:
      attached_reqs = FILTER (FR + CR) WHERE acceptance_criteria contains crt.criterion
      criteria_coverage.append({
        criterion_id: crt.id,
        criterion: crt.criterion,
        attached_to: [r.id FOR r IN attached_reqs]
      })

4.3 EXECUTE validation rules:

    errors = []
    warnings = []
    
    # V1: All stakeholder needs addressed
    FOR EACH cov IN stakeholder_coverage:
      IF len(cov.need_requirements) == 0:
        errors.append("V1: Stakeholder " + cov.stakeholder_id + " need not addressed")
    
    # V2: All stakeholder concerns addressed
    FOR EACH cov IN stakeholder_coverage:
      IF len(cov.concern_requirements) == 0:
        warnings.append("V2: Stakeholder " + cov.stakeholder_id + " concern not addressed")
    
    # V3: All hard constraints have CR
    FOR EACH cov IN constraint_coverage:
      IF len(cov.requirements) == 0:
        errors.append("V3: Constraint " + cov.constraint_id + " has no CR")
    
    # V4: All scope items have FR
    FOR EACH cov IN scope_coverage:
      IF len(cov.requirements) == 0:
        errors.append("V4: Scope item " + cov.scope_id + " has no FR")
    
    # V5: All integration points have IR
    FOR EACH cov IN integration_coverage:
      IF len(cov.requirements) == 0:
        errors.append("V5: Integration " + cov.integration_id + " has no IR")
    
    # V6: All requirements have testability
    FOR EACH req IN (FR + NFR + CR + IR):
      IF req.testability.method == null:
        errors.append("V6: Requirement " + req.id + " has no testability")
    
    # V7: All requirements have component
    FOR EACH req IN (FR + NFR + CR + IR):
      IF len(req.component) == 0:
        errors.append("V7: Requirement " + req.id + " has no component")
    
    # V8: No duplicate statements
    statements = [req.statement FOR req IN (FR + NFR + CR + IR)]
    IF len(statements) != len(unique(statements)):
      errors.append("V8: Duplicate requirement statements found")
    
    # V9: No contradictions (manual check flagged)
    # V10: All CR have priority must
    FOR EACH cr IN CR:
      IF cr.priority != "must":
        errors.append("V10: CR " + cr.id + " priority is not must")
    
    # V11: All requirements have rationale
    FOR EACH req IN (FR + NFR + CR + IR):
      IF req.rationale == null:
        warnings.append("V11: Requirement " + req.id + " has no rationale")
    
    # V12: All criteria attached
    FOR EACH cov IN criteria_coverage:
      IF len(cov.attached_to) == 0:
        warnings.append("V12: Criterion " + cov.criterion_id + " not attached")
    
    # V13/V14: Count checks
    total = len(FR) + len(NFR) + len(CR) + len(IR)
    IF total < 20:
      warnings.append("V13: Only " + total + " requirements (expected ≥20)")
    IF total > 60:
      warnings.append("V14: " + total + " requirements (expected ≤60)")

4.4 CALCULATE summary:

    coverage_summary = {
      stakeholders: {
        total: 5,
        needs_addressed: count WHERE need_requirements.length > 0,
        concerns_addressed: count WHERE concern_requirements.length > 0,
        fully_covered: count WHERE both > 0
      },
      hard_constraints: {
        total: 6,
        with_requirements: count WHERE requirements.length > 0
      },
      scope_items: {
        total: 10,
        with_requirements: count WHERE requirements.length > 0
      },
      integration_points: {
        total: 6,
        with_requirements: count WHERE requirements.length > 0
      },
      success_criteria: {
        total: 7,
        attached: count WHERE attached_to.length > 0
      }
    }
    
    summary = {
      total_requirements: len(FR) + len(NFR) + len(CR) + len(IR),
      by_category: {
        functional: len(FR),
        non_functional: len(NFR),
        constraint: len(CR),
        interface: len(IR)
      },
      by_priority: {
        must: count WHERE priority == "must",
        should: count WHERE priority == "should",
        could: count WHERE priority == "could"
      },
      by_component: {
        Frontend: count WHERE component contains "Frontend",
        API: count WHERE component contains "API",
        Orchestration: count WHERE component contains "Orchestration",
        Persistence: count WHERE component contains "Persistence",
        LLM: count WHERE component contains "LLM",
        Observability: count WHERE component contains "Observability"
      }
    }

4.5 DECISION:

    IF len(errors) > 0:
      LOG errors
      TRANSITION → DERIVING  # Remediate
    ELSE:
      LOG warnings
      TRANSITION → REVIEWING
```

---

### Phase 5: Human Review [REVIEWING]

**Entry:** Validation passed

**Human Interaction:** Required (gate)

**Procedure:**
```
5.1 PREPARE presentation:

    Present Instance1Step2Output:
    
    - Functional Requirements (table: id, statement, priority, component)
    - Non-Functional Requirements (table: id, statement, subcategory, priority, metric)
    - Constraint Requirements (table: id, statement, subcategory, violation)
    - Interface Requirements (table: id, statement, from, to, protocol)
    
    - Stakeholder Coverage (table: stakeholder, need addressed, concern addressed)
    - Constraint Coverage (table: constraint, requirements)
    - Scope Coverage (table: scope item, requirements)
    - Integration Coverage (table: integration, requirements)
    - Criteria Attachment (table: criterion, attached to)
    
    - Summary (totals, by category, by priority, by component)
    - Validation Results (errors, warnings)

5.2 REQUEST decision: APPROVE | REJECT | MODIFY

5.3 WAIT for human response

5.4 PROCESS decision:

    IF APPROVE:
      TRANSITION → COMPLETE
    
    IF REJECT:
      RECEIVE feedback
      LOG feedback
      TRANSITION → DERIVING
    
    IF MODIFY:
      RECEIVE modifications
      APPLY modifications
      TRANSITION → VALIDATING
```

---

**Entry:** Approved

**Procedure:**
```
6.1 SET metadata.state = "COMPLETE"

6.2 RECORD approval:
    metadata.approval = {
      approver: [human],
      timestamp: now(),
      decision: "APPROVE"
    }

6.3 PACKAGE Instance1Step2Output

6.4 EMIT for Instance 1 Step 3

6.5 LOG completion

Phase 3: Requirement Derivation [DERIVING]
3.1 Functional Requirements (FR)
FR-001: Workflow Step Execution
Field	Value
Statement	The system shall execute the 10-step structured reasoning workflow in sequence, where each step produces defined outputs that feed into subsequent steps.
Priority	must
Source	core_problem, IN-001
Component	Orchestration
Rationale	Core capability; the system exists to execute this workflow
Testability	demonstration: Execute complete workflow from Step 1 to Step 10
Acceptance Criteria	CRT-001: All 10 steps executable end-to-end
FR-002: Two-Pass Execution Model
Field	Value
Statement	The system shall implement a two-pass execution model for each step: Pass 1 (Definition) creates methodology documents autonomously, Pass 2 (Execution) produces deliverables under human supervision.
Priority	must
Source	core_problem, IN-002
Component	Orchestration
Rationale	Fundamental architectural pattern enabling methodology-before-execution
Testability	demonstration: Execute both passes for a step, verify distinct behaviors
Acceptance Criteria	CRT-002: Pass 1 runs autonomously without gates
FR-003: Human Approval Gates
Field	Value
Statement	The system shall implement human approval gates at the end of each Pass 2 step, blocking advancement until explicit approval is received.
Priority	must
Source	core_problem, IN-003
Component	Orchestration, API, Frontend
Rationale	Ensures human oversight at critical decision points
Testability	test: Attempt to advance without approval, verify blocked
Acceptance Criteria	CRT-003: Pass 2 gates block without approval
FR-004: Workflow State Persistence
Field	Value
Statement	The system shall persist workflow state to PostgreSQL, including current position, accumulated outputs, and conversation history.
Priority	must
Source	IN-004
Component	Persistence, Orchestration
Rationale	Enables recovery, audit, and continuity across sessions
Testability	test: Save state, terminate, restore, verify complete state
Acceptance Criteria	CRT-004: State recoverable from checkpoint
FR-005: LangGraph State Machine
Field	Value
Statement	The system shall use LangGraph to implement the workflow state machine, managing step sequencing, pass transitions, and interrupt handling.
Priority	must
Source	IN-005
Component	Orchestration
Rationale	LangGraph provides required state machine and checkpoint capabilities
Testability	inspection: Verify LangGraph graph structure matches workflow
FR-006: Checkpoint Recovery

Statement	The system shall support recovery from any LangGraph checkpoint, restoring full workflow state including conversation history.
Priority	must
Source	IN-004, IN-005
Component	Orchestration, Persistence
Rationale	Prevents work loss on failure; enables resumption
Testability	test: Checkpoint, simulate failure, restore, verify state
Acceptance Criteria	CRT-004: State recoverable from checkpoint
FR-007: REST API Endpoints

Statement	The system shall expose FastAPI REST endpoints for workflow initialization, step advancement, human decisions, and state queries.
Priority	must
Source	IN-006
Component	API
Rationale	Provides programmatic interface for frontend and external systems
Testability	test: Call each endpoint, verify correct response
FR-008: SSE Response Streaming

FR-015: Traceability Chain Storage

FR-017: Step Output Review Interface

Priority	must
Source	SH-002 (need)
Component	Frontend, API
Rationale	Reviewers need adequate information to make good decisions
Testability	inspection: Verify review interface shows complete context

Guidance Document V1
Considering: Instance 1 Step 2 Output + Data Sheet V1 + To Do List V1

Context
Instance 1 Step 3 transforms software requirements into objectives. The Requirements Package defined:

23 Functional Requirements (FR)
11 Non-Functional Requirements (NFR)
6 Constraint Requirements (CR)
6 Interface Requirements (IR)
Total: 46 requirements
Software-Specific Considerations
Objective Categories for Software:
CAP (Capability): What the system achieves (from FR)
QUAL (Quality): What quality levels are achieved (from NFR)
COMP (Compliance): What constraints are satisfied (from CR)
INTF (Interface): What integrations work correctly (from IR)
Transformation Pattern:
Requirement: "The system shall X"
Objective: "X is achieved successfully"
Success Criteria: Definition + Verification
Consolidation Opportunity:
Multiple requirements may define ONE success condition
Example: FR-001, FR-002, FR-003 all relate to "workflow execution works"
Consolidate into single capability objective with multiple verification points
Priority Mapping:
must requirement → primary objective
should requirement → secondary objective
could requirement → tertiary objective
Principles
Objectives state achievement, not action ("achieved" not "shall")
Every objective must be verifiable
Every objective traces to ≥1 requirement
Consolidate where appropriate to avoid redundancy
Success criteria have two parts: definition (what) and verification (how)
Detailed Procedure V1
Considering: Data Sheet V1 + To Do List V1 + Guidance Document V1

Phase 1: Reception [RECEIVED]
INPUT: Instance 1 Step 2 Output

ACTION:
  - Verify state = COMPLETE
  - Verify approval present
  - Load all requirement categories

OUTPUT: Validated input
TRANSITION → ANALYZING
Phase 2: Requirement Analysis [ANALYZING]
INPUT: Requirements (FR, NFR, CR, IR)

2.1 GROUP requirements by category
2.2 IDENTIFY related requirements (candidates for consolidation)
2.3 EXTRACT success conditions from each requirement
2.4 MAP requirements to objective categories:
    FR → CAP
    NFR → QUAL
    CR → COMP
    IR → INTF

OUTPUT: Grouped requirements with success conditions
TRANSITION → TRANSFORMING
Phase 3: Objective Derivation [TRANSFORMING]
INPUT: Grouped requirements

FOR EACH requirement group:
  IF consolidation appropriate:
    objective = derive_consolidated_objective(group)
  ELSE:
    FOR EACH requirement:
      objective = derive_objective(requirement)
  
  objective.success_criteria = {
    definition: what_success_looks_like,
    verification: how_to_verify
  }
  
  objective.priority = map_priority(requirement.priority)

OUTPUT: Draft objectives
TRANSITION → VALIDATING
Phase 4: Framework Construction [VALIDATING]
INPUT: Draft objectives

4.1 BUILD success framework:
    minimum_viable = objectives WHERE priority == primary
    target = minimum_viable + objectives WHERE priority == secondary
    aspirational = target + objectives WHERE priority == tertiary

4.2 VALIDATE:
    - Every requirement → ≥1 objective
    - Every objective is SARV (Specific, Achievable, Relevant, Verifiable)
    - No orphan objectives
    - Framework is coherent

IF validation fails:
  TRANSITION → TRANSFORMING (remediate)
ELSE:
  TRANSITION → REVIEWING
Phase 5: Human Review [REVIEWING]
INPUT: Validated objectives

ACTION:
  - Present objectives by category
  - Present success framework
  - Present traceability
  - Request approval

WAIT for decision

IF APPROVE: TRANSITION → COMPLETE
IF REJECT: TRANSITION → TRANSFORMING with feedback
Phase 6: Finalization [COMPLETE]
INPUT: Approved objectives

ACTION:
  - Package output
  - Record approval
  - Emit for downstream use

    capability:  # CAP-NNN
      - id: CAP-NNN
        statement: string        # Achievement formulation
        category: capability
        priority: primary | secondary | tertiary
        success_criteria:
          definition: string     # What success looks like
          verification:
            method: demonstration | test | inspection | analysis
            description: string
            evidence: string     # What proves achievement
        traces_to:
          requirements: [FR-NNN]
          consolidated: boolean  # True if multiple requirements
        component: [string]      # Components involved
    
    quality:  # QUAL-NNN
      - id: QUAL-NNN
        statement: string
        category: quality
        subcategory: performance | reliability | usability | maintainability | observability
        priority: primary | secondary | tertiary
        success_criteria:
          definition: string
          verification:
            method: test | analysis | inspection
            description: string
            evidence: string
          metric: string | null  # Quantitative if applicable
          threshold: string | null
        traces_to:
          requirements: [NFR-NNN]
          consolidated: boolean
        component: [string]
    
    compliance:  # COMP-NNN
      - id: COMP-NNN
        statement: string
        category: compliance
        subcategory: technology | architecture | behavior | integration
        priority: primary        # Always primary (from must)
        success_criteria:
          definition: string
          verification:
            method: inspection | test | analysis
            description: string
            evidence: string
        traces_to:
          requirements: [CR-NNN]
          consolidated: boolean
        component: [string]
        non_compliance_consequence: string
    
    interface:  # INTF-NNN
      - id: INTF-NNN
        statement: string
        category: interface
        priority: primary | secondary
        success_criteria:
          definition: string
          verification:
            method: test | demonstration
            description: string
            evidence: string
        traces_to:
          requirements: [IR-NNN]
          consolidated: boolean
        from_component: string
        to_component: string
        protocol: string
  
  success_framework:
    minimum_viable:
      description: "Must achieve for system to be viable"
      objectives: [id]
      requirement_coverage: [id]
    target:
      description: "Expected full success state"
      objectives: [id]
      requirement_coverage: [id]
    aspirational:
      description: "Exceeds expectations"
      objectives: [id]
      requirement_coverage: [id]
  
  traceability:
    forward:  # objective → requirements
      - objective_id: string
        requirements: [string]
        consolidated: boolean
    
    backward:  # requirement → objective
      - requirement_id: string
        objective_id: string
    
    coverage:
      requirements_total: number
      requirements_covered: number
      coverage_percentage: number
  
  summary:
    total_objectives: number
    by_category:
      capability: number
      quality: number
      compliance: number
      interface: number
    by_priority:
      primary: number
      secondary: number
      tertiary: number
    consolidation:
      requirements_input: number
      objectives_output: number
      consolidation_ratio: number
```

### Validation Rules

| ID | Rule | Severity |
|----|------|----------|
| V1 | Every FR → ≥1 CAP | Error |
| V2 | Every NFR → ≥1 QUAL | Error |
| V3 | Every CR → ≥1 COMP | Error |
| V4 | Every IR → ≥1 INTF | Error |
| V5 | All objectives have success_criteria.definition | Error |
| V6 | All objectives have success_criteria.verification | Error |
| V7 | All COMP objectives have priority = primary | Error |
| V8 | All objectives have traces_to with ≥1 requirement | Error |
| V9 | Minimum viable framework has ≥1 objective | Error |
| V10 | No orphan objectives (all trace to requirements) | Error |
| V11 | Objectives are SARV (Specific, Achievable, Relevant, Verifiable) | Warning |
| V12 | Consolidation preserves all source requirement IDs | Error |

### Priority Mapping

| Requirement Priority | Objective Priority | Framework Level |
|---------------------|-------------------|-----------------|
| must | primary | Minimum Viable |
| should | secondary | Target |
| could | tertiary | Aspirational |

### Verification Method Taxonomy

| Method | When to Use | Evidence Type |
|--------|-------------|---------------|
| **demonstration** | Observable behavior | Video, walkthrough, log |
| **test** | Measurable outcome | Test results, metrics |
| **inspection** | Static verification | Code review, config check |
| **analysis** | Derived conclusion | Report, calculation |

### State Definitions

| State | Description | Human Interaction |
|-------|-------------|-------------------|
| RECEIVED | Step 2 output received | None |
| ANALYZING | Grouping and analyzing requirements | None |
| TRANSFORMING | Deriving objectives | None |
| VALIDATING | Checking coverage and quality | None |
| REVIEWING | Awaiting approval | Required (gate) |
| COMPLETE | Approved and packaged | None |

## Guidance Document V2
*Considering: Data Sheet V2 + To Do List V2 + Detailed Procedure V1*

### Position in Workflow

Instance 1 Step 3 transforms software requirements into objectives:

| Step | Input | Output | Purpose |
|------|-------|--------|---------|
| Step 1 | Architecture Spec | Problem Statement | Define what to build |
| Step 2 | Problem Statement | Requirements | Define what it must do |
| **Step 3** | **Requirements** | **Objectives** | **Define what success looks like** |

### Inheritance from Instance 0

Instance 0 Step 3 established:
- Objective schema (id, statement, category, priority, success_criteria, traces_to)
- Transformation from requirements to objectives
- Success framework (minimum viable, target, aspirational)
- SARV quality criteria (Specific, Achievable, Relevant, Verifiable)
- Consolidation guidance

Instance 1 **specializes** these:
- Adds Interface Objectives (INTF) category
- Adds software-specific verification methods
- Adds component mapping
- Adds metric/threshold for quality objectives

### Objective Categories for Software

| Category | Prefix | Derived From | Question Answered |
|----------|--------|--------------|-------------------|
| Capability | CAP | FR | What does the system achieve? |
| Quality | QUAL | NFR | What quality levels are achieved? |
| Compliance | COMP | CR | What constraints are satisfied? |
| Interface | INTF | IR | What integrations work correctly? |

### Transformation Patterns

**Pattern 1: FR → Capability Objective**
```
Requirement: FR-001
"The system shall execute the 10-step structured reasoning workflow..."

Transformation:
  Statement: "10-step workflow execution is achieved"
  Success Criteria:
    Definition: All 10 steps can be executed in sequence, producing correct outputs
    Verification:
      Method: demonstration
      Description: Execute complete workflow from Step 1 to Step 10
      Evidence: Workflow completion log with all step outputs
```

**Pattern 2: NFR → Quality Objective**
```
Requirement: NFR-001
"The system shall begin streaming responses within 500ms..."

Transformation:
  Statement: "Response streaming latency target is achieved"
  Success Criteria:
    Definition: First byte of streaming response delivered within latency threshold
    Verification:
      Method: test
      Description: Measure time from generation start to first byte under load
      Evidence: Performance test results showing p95 latency
    Metric: First byte latency
    Threshold: ≤ 500ms (p95)
```

**Pattern 3: CR → Compliance Objective**
```
Requirement: CR-004
"The system shall prevent any Pass 2 step from advancing without approval..."

Transformation:
  Statement: "Approval gate enforcement is achieved"
  Success Criteria:
    Definition: No Pass 2 step advances without explicit human approval
    Verification:
      Method: test
      Description: Attempt programmatic advance without approval
      Evidence: Test results showing blocked attempts
  Non-compliance Consequence: Human oversight bypassed; trust violation
```

**Pattern 4: IR → Interface Objective**
```
Requirement: IR-002
"The API shall stream responses to frontend using SSE..."

Transformation:
  Statement: "API-to-Frontend streaming integration is achieved"
  Success Criteria:
    Definition: Real-time streaming from API to Frontend works correctly
    Verification:
      Method: test
      Description: Connect SSE, verify events received in order
      Evidence: Integration test results
```

### Consolidation Guidelines

**When to Consolidate:**
- Multiple requirements describe ONE success condition
- Example: FR-001, FR-002, FR-003 all relate to "workflow execution works correctly"
- Consolidate into: CAP-001 "Complete workflow execution is achieved"
- Traces to: [FR-001, FR-002, FR-003]

**When NOT to Consolidate:**
- Requirements describe genuinely distinct success conditions
- Consolidation would create compound objectives
- Traceability would become obscured

**Always:**
- Preserve all source requirement IDs in traces_to
- Mark consolidated = true

### Priority Mapping

| Requirement Priority | Objective Priority | Framework Level | Meaning |
|---------------------|-------------------|-----------------|---------|
| must | primary | Minimum Viable | System fails without |
| should | secondary | Target | System disappoints without |
| could | tertiary | Aspirational | Nice to have |

### Success Framework
```
MINIMUM VIABLE
├── All primary objectives
├── System is usable but basic
└── Derived from: must requirements

TARGET STATE
├── All primary + secondary objectives
├── System meets full expectations
└── Derived from: must + should requirements

ASPIRATIONAL
├── All objectives
├── System exceeds expectations
└── Derived from: all requirements
```

### Verification Methods for Software

| Method | Description | When to Use | Evidence |
|--------|-------------|-------------|----------|
| **demonstration** | Show it working | Observable behavior | Recording, walkthrough |
| **test** | Automated verification | Measurable outcomes | Test results |
| **inspection** | Manual review | Static properties | Review checklist |
| **analysis** | Reasoned conclusion | Derived properties | Analysis report |

### Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Symptom | Remedy |
|--------------|---------|--------|
| Requirement as objective | "System shall X" | Rephrase as "X is achieved" |
| Unverifiable objective | No clear verification | Add specific evidence |
| Over-consolidation | Loss of traceability | Keep distinct where needed |
| Under-consolidation | Redundant objectives | Merge related successes |
| Missing framework level | No aspirational objectives | Map all priorities |

---

## Detailed Procedure V2
*Considering: Data Sheet V2 + To Do List V2 + Guidance Document V2*

### State Machine
```
STATES = {RECEIVED, ANALYZING, TRANSFORMING, VALIDATING, REVIEWING, COMPLETE}
INITIAL = RECEIVED
TERMINAL = COMPLETE

TRANSITIONS:
  RECEIVED      → ANALYZING     : input_valid()
  ANALYZING     → TRANSFORMING  : analysis_complete()
  TRANSFORMING  → VALIDATING    : objectives_complete()
  VALIDATING    → TRANSFORMING  : validation_failed()
  VALIDATING    → REVIEWING     : validation_passed()
  REVIEWING     → TRANSFORMING  : rejected(feedback)
  REVIEWING     → VALIDATING    : modify(changes)
  REVIEWING     → COMPLETE      : approved()

HUMAN_GATES = {REVIEWING}
```

### Phase 1: Reception [RECEIVED]
```
INPUT: Instance 1 Step 2 Output

1.1 VERIFY input:
    ASSERT step_2_output.state == COMPLETE
    ASSERT step_2_output.approval.decision == APPROVE

1.2 LOAD requirements:
    FR = step_2_output.requirements.functional      # 23
    NFR = step_2_output.requirements.non_functional # 11
    CR = step_2_output.requirements.constraint      # 6
    IR = step_2_output.requirements.interface       # 6
    
    total_requirements = 46

1.3 LOAD context:
    step_1_output = load(Instance 1 Step 1 Output)  # For stakeholder reference

1.4 INITIALIZE output structure

1.5 SET state: ANALYZING

TRANSITION → ANALYZING
```

### Phase 2: Requirement Analysis [ANALYZING]
```
INPUT: All requirements (FR, NFR, CR, IR)

2.1 GROUP FR by functional area:

    fr_groups = {
      "workflow_execution": [FR-001, FR-002, FR-003],
      "state_management": [FR-004, FR-005, FR-006],
      "api_layer": [FR-007, FR-008],
      "user_interface": [FR-009, FR-010],
      "document_system": [FR-011, FR-012],
      "instance_management": [FR-013, FR-014],
      "traceability": [FR-015, FR-016],
      "human_interaction": [FR-017, FR-018, FR-019],
      "operations": [FR-020, FR-021, FR-022, FR-023]
    }

2.2 IDENTIFY consolidation candidates:

    FOR EACH group IN fr_groups:
      IF all requirements in group share success condition:
        mark_for_consolidation(group)
      ELSE:
        mark_for_individual(group)

2.3 GROUP NFR by subcategory:

    nfr_groups = {
      "performance": [NFR-001],
      "usability": [NFR-002, NFR-003],
      "reliability": [NFR-004, NFR-006, NFR-009, NFR-010],
      "maintainability": [NFR-005, NFR-007, NFR-008, NFR-011]
    }

2.4 ANALYZE CR (generally no consolidation):

    cr_list = [CR-001, CR-002, CR-003, CR-004, CR-005, CR-006]
    # Each constraint is distinct

2.5 GROUP IR by integration area:

    ir_groups = {
      "frontend_api": [IR-001, IR-002],
      "api_orchestration": [IR-003],
      "orchestration_persistence": [IR-004],
      "orchestration_llm": [IR-005],
      "observability": [IR-006]
    }

TRANSITION → TRANSFORMING
```

### Phase 3: Objective Derivation [TRANSFORMING]
```
INPUT: Grouped and analyzed requirements

3.1 DERIVE Capability Objectives:

    cap_counter = 0
    CAP = []
    
    FOR EACH group IN fr_groups:
      IF marked_for_consolidation(group):
        cap_counter++
        cap = derive_consolidated_cap(group, cap_counter)
        CAP.append(cap)
      ELSE:
        FOR EACH fr IN group:
          cap_counter++
          cap = derive_individual_cap(fr, cap_counter)
          CAP.append(cap)

3.2 DERIVE Quality Objectives:

    qual_counter = 0
    QUAL = []
    
    FOR EACH nfr IN NFR:
      qual_counter++
      qual = {
        id: "QUAL-" + pad(qual_counter, 3),
        statement: transform_to_achievement(nfr.statement),
        category: "quality",
        subcategory: nfr.subcategory,
        priority: map_priority(nfr.priority),
        success_criteria: {
          definition: derive_definition(nfr),
          verification: {
            method: determine_method(nfr),
            description: derive_verification(nfr),
            evidence: derive_evidence(nfr)
          },
          metric: nfr.metric,
          threshold: derive_threshold(nfr)
        },
        traces_to: {
          requirements: [nfr.id],
          consolidated: false
        },
        component: nfr.component
      }
      QUAL.append(qual)

3.3 DERIVE Compliance Objectives:

    comp_counter = 0
    COMP = []
    
    FOR EACH cr IN CR:
      comp_counter++
      comp = {
        id: "COMP-" + pad(comp_counter, 3),
        statement: transform_to_achievement(cr.statement),
        category: "compliance",
        subcategory: cr.subcategory,
        priority: "primary",  # Always primary for constraints
        success_criteria: {
          definition: derive_definition(cr),
          verification: {
            method: determine_method(cr),
            description: derive_verification(cr),
            evidence: derive_evidence(cr)
          }
        },
        traces_to: {
          requirements: [cr.id],
          consolidated: false
        },
        component: cr.component,
        non_compliance_consequence: cr.violation_consequence
      }
      COMP.append(comp)

3.4 DERIVE Interface Objectives:

    intf_counter = 0
    INTF = []
    
    FOR EACH ir IN IR:
      intf_counter++
      intf = {
        id: "INTF-" + pad(intf_counter, 3),
        statement: transform_to_achievement(ir.statement),
        category: "interface",
        priority: map_priority(ir.priority),
        success_criteria: {
          definition: derive_definition(ir),
          verification: {
            method: "test",
            description: derive_verification(ir),
            evidence: derive_evidence(ir)
          }
        },
        traces_to: {
          requirements: [ir.id],
          consolidated: false
        },
        from_component: ir.from_component,
        to_component: ir.to_component,
        protocol: ir.protocol
      }
      INTF.append(intf)

TRANSITION → VALIDATING
```

### Phase 4: Validation [VALIDATING]
```
INPUT: Derived objectives (CAP, QUAL, COMP, INTF)

4.1 BUILD success framework:

    primary_objectives = FILTER all WHERE priority == "primary"
    secondary_objectives = FILTER all WHERE priority == "secondary"
    tertiary_objectives = FILTER all WHERE priority == "tertiary"
    
    success_framework = {
      minimum_viable: {
        description: "Must achieve for system to be viable",
        objectives: [o.id FOR o IN primary_objectives],
        requirement_coverage: flatten([o.traces_to.requirements FOR o IN primary_objectives])
      },
      target: {
        description: "Expected full success state",
        objectives: [o.id FOR o IN (primary_objectives + secondary_objectives)],
        requirement_coverage: flatten(...)
      },
      aspirational: {
        description: "Exceeds expectations",
        objectives: [o.id FOR o IN all_objectives],
        requirement_coverage: flatten(...)
      }
    }

4.2 BUILD traceability:

    forward_trace = []
    FOR EACH obj IN all_objectives:
      forward_trace.append({
        objective_id: obj.id,
        requirements: obj.traces_to.requirements,
        consolidated: obj.traces_to.consolidated
      })
    
    backward_trace = []
    FOR EACH req IN all_requirements:
      matching_obj = FIND objective WHERE req.id IN traces_to.requirements
      backward_trace.append({
        requirement_id: req.id,
        objective_id: matching_obj.id
      })

4.3 EXECUTE validation rules:

    V1: ∀ FR: ∃ CAP WHERE FR.id IN CAP.traces_to
    V2: ∀ NFR: ∃ QUAL WHERE NFR.id IN QUAL.traces_to
    V3: ∀ CR: ∃ COMP WHERE CR.id IN COMP.traces_to
    V4: ∀ IR: ∃ INTF WHERE IR.id IN INTF.traces_to
    V5: ∀ objective: success_criteria.definition ≠ null
    V6: ∀ objective: success_criteria.verification ≠ null
    V7: ∀ COMP: priority == "primary"
    V8: ∀ objective: len(traces_to.requirements) ≥ 1
    V9: len(minimum_viable.objectives) ≥ 1
    V10: ∀ objective: len(traces_to.requirements) ≥ 1 (no orphans)
    V11: ∀ objective: is_SARV(objective)
    V12: ∀ consolidated objective: all source IDs preserved

4.4 CALCULATE summary:

    summary = {
      total_objectives: len(CAP) + len(QUAL) + len(COMP) + len(INTF),
      by_category: {...},
      by_priority: {...},
      consolidation: {
        requirements_input: 46,
        objectives_output: total_objectives,
        consolidation_ratio: 46 / total_objectives
      }
    }

4.5 DECISION:

    IF errors:
      TRANSITION → TRANSFORMING
    ELSE:
      TRANSITION → REVIEWING
```

### Phase 5: Human Review [REVIEWING]
```
INPUT: Validated objectives

5.1 PRESENT:
    - Capability Objectives (table)
    - Quality Objectives (table)
    - Compliance Objectives (table)
    - Interface Objectives (table)
    - Success Framework (3 levels)
    - Traceability summary
    - Coverage statistics
    - Consolidation summary

5.2 REQUEST decision: APPROVE | REJECT | MODIFY

5.3 WAIT for human response

5.4 PROCESS decision
```

### Phase 6: Finalization [COMPLETE]
```
INPUT: Approved objectives

6.1 SET state: COMPLETE
6.2 RECORD approval
6.3 PACKAGE Instance1Step3Output
6.4 EMIT for downstream use
V3: FINAL REFINEMENT
Data Sheet V3
Considering: Detailed Procedure V2 + Guidance Document V2 + To Do List V2

Input Specification
Required Inputs:

Input	Source	Description	Validation
Instance 0 Step 3 V3 Methodology	Inherited	Abstract methodology	4 documents present
Instance 1 Step 2 Output	Prior Step	Requirements	State=COMPLETE, approved
Input Field Requirements:

Field	Type	Count	Required For
requirements.functional	array	23	CAP derivation
requirements.non_functional	array	11	QUAL derivation
requirements.constraint	array	6	COMP derivation
requirements.interface	array	6	INTF derivation
Input Validation Gate:

Instance 1 Step 2 state = COMPLETE
Instance 1 Step 2 approval = APPROVE
All 4 requirement categories present
Total requirements = 46
Output Specification
yaml
Instance1Step3Output:
  metadata:
    instance: 1
    level: implementation
    step: 3
    step_name: "Objectives"
    state: COMPLETE
    timestamp: datetime
    input_references:
      instance_0_methodology: reference
      instance_1_step_2: reference
      instance_1_step_1: reference
    approval:
      approver: string
      timestamp: datetime
      decision: APPROVE
  
  objectives:
    capability:
      - id: CAP-NNN
        statement: string
        category: capability
        priority: primary | secondary | tertiary
        success_criteria:
          definition: string
          verification:
            method: demonstration | test | inspection | analysis
            description: string
            evidence: string
        traces_to:
          requirements: [string]
          consolidated: boolean
        component: [string]
        acceptance_criteria: [string]  # From Step 1 CRT if applicable
    
    quality:
      - id: QUAL-NNN
        statement: string
        category: quality
        subcategory: performance | reliability | usability | maintainability | observability
        priority: primary | secondary | tertiary
        success_criteria:
          definition: string
          verification:
            method: test | analysis | inspection
            description: string
            evidence: string
          metric: string | null
          threshold: string | null
        traces_to:
          requirements: [string]
          consolidated: boolean
        component: [string]
    
    compliance:
      - id: COMP-NNN
        statement: string
        category: compliance
        subcategory: technology | architecture | behavior | integration
        priority: primary
        success_criteria:
          definition: string
          verification:
            method: inspection | test | analysis
            description: string
            evidence: string
        traces_to:
          requirements: [string]
          consolidated: boolean
        component: [string]
        non_compliance_consequence: string
    
    interface:
      - id: INTF-NNN
        statement: string
        category: interface
        priority: primary | secondary
        success_criteria:
          definition: string
          verification:
            method: test | demonstration
            description: string
            evidence: string
        traces_to:
          requirements: [string]
          consolidated: boolean
        from_component: string
        to_component: string
        protocol: string
  
  success_framework:
    minimum_viable:
      description: string
      criteria: string
      objectives: [string]
      objective_count: number
      requirement_coverage:
        count: number
        ids: [string]
    target:
      description: string
      criteria: string
      objectives: [string]
      objective_count: number
      requirement_coverage:
        count: number
        ids: [string]
    aspirational:
      description: string
      criteria: string
      objectives: [string]
      objective_count: number
      requirement_coverage:
        count: number
        ids: [string]
  
  traceability:
    forward:
      - objective_id: string
        category: string
        requirements: [string]
        consolidated: boolean
    backward:
      functional:
        - requirement_id: string
          objective_id: string
      non_functional:
        - requirement_id: string
          objective_id: string
      constraint:
        - requirement_id: string
          objective_id: string
      interface:
        - requirement_id: string
          objective_id: string
    coverage:
      functional:
        total: 23
        covered: number
        percentage: number
      non_functional:
        total: 11
        covered: number
        percentage: number
      constraint:
        total: 6
        covered: number
        percentage: number
      interface:
        total: 6
        covered: number
        percentage: number
      overall:
        total: 46
        covered: number
        percentage: number
  
  summary:
    total_objectives: number
    by_category:
      capability: number
      quality: number
      compliance: number
      interface: number
    by_priority:
      primary: number
      secondary: number
      tertiary: number
    consolidation:
      requirements_input: 46
      objectives_output: number
      consolidation_ratio: number
      consolidated_objectives: number
```

### Validation Rules

| ID | Rule | Severity | Remediation |
|----|------|----------|-------------|
| V1 | ∀ FR: ∃ CAP with FR in traces_to | Error | Add CAP for uncovered FR |
| V2 | ∀ NFR: ∃ QUAL with NFR in traces_to | Error | Add QUAL for uncovered NFR |
| V3 | ∀ CR: ∃ COMP with CR in traces_to | Error | Add COMP for uncovered CR |
| V4 | ∀ IR: ∃ INTF with IR in traces_to | Error | Add INTF for uncovered IR |
| V5 | ∀ objective: definition ≠ null ∧ ≠ "" | Error | Add definition |
| V6 | ∀ objective: verification.method ≠ null | Error | Add verification method |
| V7 | ∀ COMP: priority = primary | Error | Set priority to primary |
| V8 | ∀ objective: traces_to.requirements.length ≥ 1 | Error | Add requirement trace |
| V9 | minimum_viable.objectives.length ≥ 1 | Error | Ensure primary objectives exist |
| V10 | ∀ objective: is traceable (no orphans) | Error | Remove or add trace |
| V11 | ∀ objective: is SARV | Warning | Improve objective quality |
| V12 | ∀ consolidated: all source IDs in traces_to | Error | Add missing source IDs |
| V13 | coverage.overall.percentage = 100% | Error | Cover all requirements |

### Quality Criteria (SARV)

| Criterion | Test | Example Pass | Example Fail |
|-----------|------|--------------|--------------|
| **Specific** | Single interpretation | "Workflow executes all 10 steps" | "Workflow works" |
| **Achievable** | Realistic | "Response in 500ms" | "Response in 0ms" |
| **Relevant** | Traces to requirement | Has traces_to | No traces_to |
| **Verifiable** | Clear evidence | "Test results show..." | "Somehow verified" |

### State Definitions

| State | Entry | Exit | Human |
|-------|-------|------|-------|
| RECEIVED | Step 2 output provided | Input validated | None |
| ANALYZING | Input validated | Analysis complete | None |
| TRANSFORMING | Analysis complete | Objectives derived | None |
| VALIDATING | Objectives derived | Validation passed/failed | None |
| REVIEWING | Validation passed | Decision received | Required |
| COMPLETE | Approved | Terminal | None |

## Guidance Document V3
*Considering: Data Sheet V3 + To Do List V3 + Detailed Procedure V2*

### Purpose

This document orients the agent executing Instance 1 Step 3 to derive software objectives from the approved Requirements Package.

### Position in Workflow

| Instance | Step | Input | Output |
|----------|------|-------|--------|
| Instance 0 | Step 3 | Abstract Requirements | Abstract Objectives |
| **Instance 1** | **Step 3** | **Software Requirements** | **Software Objectives** |
| Instance N | Step 3 | Domain Requirements | Domain Objectives |

Instance 1 Step 3 is the final step in the Definition Phase. After this:
- Problem Statement (Step 1) defines WHAT to build
- Requirements (Step 2) define WHAT it must do
- **Objectives (Step 3) define WHAT success looks like**

### Inheritance from Instance 0

Instance 0 Step 3 established:
- Objective schema with success_criteria
- Transformation pattern (requirement → objective)
- Success framework (minimum viable / target / aspirational)
- SARV quality criteria
- Consolidation guidelines
- Priority mapping

Instance 1 **specializes** these:
- Adds Interface Objectives (INTF) category
- Adds software verification methods
- Adds component mapping
- Adds metric/threshold for quality
- Adds non_compliance_consequence for compliance

### The Four Objective Categories

| Category | Prefix | From | Question | Example |
|----------|--------|------|----------|---------|
| Capability | CAP | FR | What is achieved? | "Workflow execution is achieved" |
| Quality | QUAL | NFR | What quality level? | "Latency target is achieved" |
| Compliance | COMP | CR | What is satisfied? | "Gate enforcement is achieved" |
| Interface | INTF | IR | What integrates? | "SSE streaming is achieved" |

### Transformation Pattern

**Requirement → Objective:**
```
REQUIREMENT (FR-001):
  "The system shall execute the 10-step structured reasoning workflow..."
  Priority: must
  Component: Orchestration

         ↓ Transform ↓

OBJECTIVE (CAP-001):
  Statement: "Complete workflow execution is achieved"
  Priority: primary (from must)
  Success Criteria:
    Definition: "All 10 workflow steps execute in sequence, each producing 
                 valid outputs that correctly feed into subsequent steps"
    Verification:
      Method: demonstration
      Description: "Execute complete workflow from Step 1 to Step 10 with 
                    a test problem"
      Evidence: "Workflow completion log showing all 10 steps with outputs"
  Traces To: [FR-001, FR-002, FR-003]  # Consolidated
  Component: [Orchestration]
```

### Consolidation Decision Logic

**Step 1: Identify Candidates**
Requirements in the same functional area that share a success condition.

**Step 2: Evaluate Consolidation**

| Question | If Yes | If No |
|----------|--------|-------|
| Do requirements share ONE success condition? | Consolidate | Keep separate |
| Would consolidation obscure traceability? | Keep separate | Consolidate |
| Would consolidation create compound objective? | Keep separate | Consolidate |

**Step 3: If Consolidating**
- Create single objective
- Include ALL source requirement IDs in traces_to
- Set consolidated = true
- Use highest priority among sources

**Consolidation Examples:**

| Group | Requirements | Consolidate? | Rationale |
|-------|--------------|--------------|-----------|
| Workflow | FR-001, FR-002, FR-003 | Yes | All define "workflow works correctly" |
| API | FR-007, FR-008 | No | REST and SSE are distinct capabilities |
| Reliability | NFR-004, NFR-006 | No | Availability and recovery time are distinct |

### Priority Mapping

| Requirement Priority | Objective Priority | Framework Level | Count (expected) |
|---------------------|-------------------|-----------------|------------------|
| must (26) | primary | Minimum Viable | ~18-20 |
| should (18) | secondary | Target | ~12-14 |
| could (2) | tertiary | Aspirational | ~2 |

### Success Framework
```
MINIMUM VIABLE (Primary Objectives)
├── System is functional but basic
├── All constraints satisfied
├── Core workflow works
├── All integrations functional
└── Requirement coverage: 26 must-priority requirements

TARGET STATE (Primary + Secondary Objectives)
├── System meets full expectations  
├── Quality attributes achieved
├── Operational features complete
└── Requirement coverage: 44 requirements (must + should)

ASPIRATIONAL (All Objectives)
├── System exceeds expectations
├── All enhancements included
└── Requirement coverage: 46 requirements (all)
```

### Verification Methods

| Method | Use For | Evidence Type |
|--------|---------|---------------|
| **demonstration** | Observable behavior (CAP) | Video, log, walkthrough |
| **test** | Measurable outcome (QUAL, INTF) | Test results, metrics |
| **inspection** | Static verification (COMP) | Review checklist, config |
| **analysis** | Derived conclusion (complex) | Analysis report |

### SARV Quality Criteria

Every objective must be:

| Criterion | Question | Fix If Failing |
|-----------|----------|----------------|
| **Specific** | Is there only one interpretation? | Add precision |
| **Achievable** | Can it be accomplished? | Reduce scope |
| **Relevant** | Does it trace to a requirement? | Add/verify trace |
| **Verifiable** | Can we prove achievement? | Add concrete evidence |

### Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Example | Remedy |
|--------------|---------|--------|
| Requirement as objective | "System shall X" | Rephrase: "X is achieved" |
| Vague success | "System works well" | Add specific criteria |
| Unverifiable | "Users are satisfied" | Add measurable evidence |
| Over-consolidation | 10 requirements → 1 objective | Keep distinct where needed |
| Missing trace | Objective without traces_to | Add requirement links |
| Wrong priority | COMP with secondary | COMP always primary |

### Acceptance Criteria Integration

Success criteria from Step 1 (CRT-001 to CRT-007) should be integrated:

| Criterion | Objective | Integration |
|-----------|-----------|-------------|
| CRT-001: 10 steps executable | CAP-001 | acceptance_criteria |
| CRT-002: Pass 1 autonomous | CAP-001 | acceptance_criteria |
| CRT-003: Gates block | COMP-004 | acceptance_criteria |
| CRT-004: Checkpoint recovery | CAP-002 | acceptance_criteria |
| CRT-005: Traceability queryable | CAP-008 | acceptance_criteria |
| CRT-006: Inheritance works | CAP-007 | acceptance_criteria |
| CRT-007: Reasoning visible | COMP-006 | acceptance_criteria |

### Quality Checklist

Before presenting for human review:

1. [ ] All FR have ≥1 CAP
2. [ ] All NFR have ≥1 QUAL
3. [ ] All CR have ≥1 COMP
4. [ ] All IR have ≥1 INTF
5. [ ] All objectives have success_criteria.definition
6. [ ] All objectives have success_criteria.verification
7. [ ] All COMP have priority = primary
8. [ ] All objectives have traces_to with ≥1 requirement
9. [ ] Success framework has all three levels
10. [ ] All consolidated objectives preserve source IDs
11. [ ] All objectives pass SARV check
12. [ ] Coverage = 100% for all requirement categories
13. [ ] Consolidation ratio is reasonable (1.5-2.5x)

## Detailed Procedure V3
*Considering: Data Sheet V3 + To Do List V3 + Guidance Document V3*

### State Machine
```
STATES = {RECEIVED, ANALYZING, TRANSFORMING, VALIDATING, REVIEWING, COMPLETE}
INITIAL = RECEIVED
TERMINAL = COMPLETE

TRANSITIONS:
  RECEIVED      → ANALYZING     : input_valid()
  ANALYZING     → TRANSFORMING  : analysis_complete()
  TRANSFORMING  → VALIDATING    : objectives_complete()
  VALIDATING    → TRANSFORMING  : validation_failed()
  VALIDATING    → REVIEWING     : validation_passed()
  REVIEWING     → TRANSFORMING  : rejected(feedback)
  REVIEWING     → VALIDATING    : modify(changes)
  REVIEWING     → COMPLETE      : approved()

HUMAN_GATES = {REVIEWING}
```

---

### Phase 1: Reception [RECEIVED]

**Entry:** Instance 1 Step 2 Output provided

**Procedure:**
```
1.1 VERIFY Step 2 Output:
    ASSERT step_2.metadata.state == "COMPLETE"
    ASSERT step_2.metadata.approval.decision == "APPROVE"

1.2 LOAD requirements:
    
    FR = step_2.requirements.functional      # 23 items
    NFR = step_2.requirements.non_functional # 11 items
    CR = step_2.requirements.constraint      # 6 items
    IR = step_2.requirements.interface       # 6 items
    
    total_requirements = 46
    
    # Priority breakdown
    must_count = 26   # 15 FR + 3 NFR + 6 CR + 2 IR (adjust based on actual)
    should_count = 18 # 8 FR + 7 NFR + 0 CR + 3 IR
    could_count = 2   # 0 FR + 1 NFR + 0 CR + 1 IR

1.3 LOAD context:
    step_1 = load("Instance 1 Step 1 Output")
    success_criteria = step_1.success_criteria  # CRT-001 to CRT-007

1.4 INITIALIZE output:
    Instance1Step3Output = {
      metadata: {...},
      objectives: {capability: [], quality: [], compliance: [], interface: []},
      success_framework: {...},
      traceability: {...},
      summary: {...}
    }

1.5 SET state: ANALYZING

TRANSITION → ANALYZING
```

---

        consolidate: false,

      },
      {
        name: "conversation_history",
        requirements: [FR-023],
        consolidate: false,
        rationale: "Distinct preservation capability"
      }
    ]

2.2 ANALYZE NFR (generally individual):

    nfr_analysis = [
      {id: NFR-001, consolidate: false},  # Performance - distinct
      {id: NFR-002, consolidate: false},  # Usability - clarity
      {id: NFR-003, consolidate: false},  # Usability - completeness
      {id: NFR-004, consolidate: false},  # Reliability - availability
      {id: NFR-005, consolidate: false},  # Maintainability - errors
